| [`method viewport_set_snap_2d_transforms_to_pixel(RID viewport, bool enabled) -> void`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/RenderingServer.xml) | [`public System.Boolean SnapTransformsToPixel { get; set; }`](../../classes/Viewport.md) | Implemented | Executable typed viewport pixel-snapping contract: false defaults, stored flags, guards, retained replay and independent transform/vertex policies. Explicit root Window construction samples active typed project defaults under the host lifecycle (ADRs 0015/0028); caller configuration takes precedence. Transform rounding applies before hierarchy composition and Y sorting; vertex rounding follows node/draw/framebuffer transforms and preserves clipped texture interpolation. CanvasPixelSnapTests and native CanvasPixelSnapRenderingTests cover negative ties, Sprite queries/retained offsets, neutral/TopLevel boundaries, scaled/nested Y groups, both flags, HLSL/GLSL and warmed allocations. Linux Wayland GPU/compatibility and dummy/software are checked; software truncation of remaining fractional vertices is an explicit ADR 0028 precision limit. Typed Viewport ownership replaces low-level RID setters; nested/offscreen viewports remain separately blocked. |

Explicit root Window construction samples active typed project defaults under the host lifecycle (ADRs 0015/0028); caller configuration takes precedence. Transform rounding applies before hierarchy composition and Y sorting; vertex rounding follows node/draw/framebuffer transforms and preserves clipped texture interpolation. CanvasPixelSnapTests and native CanvasPixelSnapRenderingTests cover negative ties, Sprite queries/retained offsets, neutral/TopLevel boundaries, scaled/nested Y groups, both flags, HLSL/GLSL and warmed allocations. Linux Wayland GPU/compatibility and dummy/software are checked; software truncation of remaining fractional vertices is an explicit ADR 0028 precision limit. Typed Viewport ownership replaces low-level RID setters; nested/offscreen viewports remain separately blocked. |

| [`property bool render_loop_enabled`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/RenderingServer.xml) | [`public System.Boolean RenderLoopEnabled { get; set; }`](../../classes/RenderingServer.md) | Partial | The Engine.Run-owned root renderer exposes finite clear-color changes, retained render-loop enablement and synchronous frame events on the owner thread. Post-draw means submission, not GPU completion/presentation; callback failures propagate after cleanup. Initial native checks exist; multiwindow/offscreen behavior and the remaining renderer surface are not implemented (ADRs 0015/0016/0028). |
